=== Project-3 Image Watermarking App (With Deployment) ===


--- Introduction to Pan Card Tempering Detector App ---

Hello all and welcome to this project on panchayat tampering detection.
In this project we will discuss tampering of panchayats using computer vision.
This project will help different organizations where their employees provide them with IDs for verification,
and the organization can know whether the ID provided by their employees is original or not.
As you know that with advent of technology, there are certain cases where users provided fake IDs or
fake Aadhaar cards, Pan cards, etc. so this is a way how you can check whether the ID provided to
you is original or not.
Currently, what the companies do, they check it with the online system.
Like if you give them the Aadhaar number, they check it online and get back to you.
But with this project, you can do it instantly.
You just have to upload the image and you can check whether the original image is matching with the
Pan card provided or not.
So let's see how we will do this.
We'll do this project in ten simple steps in which first we take the images from the user.
We'll find the threshold.
We'll find the size and the format of that image.
We'll change the shape and size of the image that we get from the user to the original Pan card image.
Then we'll convert that image to grayscale to find the similarity index.
After similarity index, we will find the threshold of the image contours and grab those contours using
im utils.
Next we will create a bounding rectangle using those counters.
After that, we'll plot difference threshold, original and tampered image, which we will compare and
get to the score of similarity to decide whether the image is tampered or not.

=== Project-11 Sentiment Analysis App (With Deployment) ===


--- Introduction ---

Hello all and welcome to this project on Dog Breed Prediction.
In this project, we will see how to use Keras and TensorFlow to build and train and test a convolutional
neural network.
This convolutional neural network that we will create will be capable of identifying the breed of a
dog, which is supplied to it as an image.
Convolutional neural networks are generally used for image classification.
This problem is also a multi-class classification problem, which is a supervised learning problem.
For this this project, the steps we will use are.
We will first load the data from Kaggle.
So I will show you how you can connect your Google Colab with Kaggle.
After that, we will load the labels that contains the image id and the breed.
We will check those breed counts.
After that we will perform one hot encodings on label of the data prediction column.
We load the images, convert them to an array, and normalize them.
Then we will check the shape and size of the x and y data and build our network architecture after that.
After building the network architecture, we will split the data and fit it into the model to create
an accuracy plot.
We evaluate the model for accuracy score and finally we will use this model for prediction.
In this project we will start by downloading the data, creating the model and finding out the predictions
using this model.
This model can be optimized using different hyperparameters, which can be used to tune this model for
a higher accuracy.
This model can be used to predict different breeds of dogs, which can be further used by different
NGOs which are working on saving animals and for educational purposes also.
So let's get started.

=== Project-19 Happiness Ranking App ===


--- Introduction to Image Watermarking App ---

Hello and welcome to this project on Image Watermarking.
In this project, we will see how we can add a watermark to an image.
So adding a watermark works as a copyright for your image so that no one else can illegally use your
image or document.
We will use OpenCV for this project to add logo as well as text as a watermark on different images.
So the steps to solve this project are.
First, we will load the image and the logo from URL.
Then we will resize those images.
We will convert those images into RGB format and then convert it to a numpy array.
Then we will store the height and width of both the image and the logo.
Finally finding out the coordinates of the center of the image.
Next we will find out the region of interest.
The region of interest is defined as the area where we need to draw our logo, where we need to put
our logo.
So we will find out that after that we will merge the logo with the image and visualize it.
Next we will create a text watermark.
We use cv2 function to define a text and its properties, and then apply it to a specific coordinate
in the image.
Then we will convert this image into RGB format and visualize it.
In this way we will complete both the image watermarking as well as text watermarking.
So adding a watermark to an image works as a copyright.
Different organizations can use it to make their content secure, so that their content cannot be misused
without their license or a paid version.
As you have seen in many firms like Microsoft or any other firm, you can see they use their watermark
so that no other company, no other person, can use that without their authorization or without a paid
version.
Also, you can see in this presentation that I have included a watermark in the left corner of my analytics.
So this is a watermark so that no one can use this PPT without a paid version or without a license.
From my analytics, we will create this project using OpenCV and Google Colab.
So let's get.

=== Project-28 Covid-19 Case Analysis ===


--- Creating and Testing the Model ---

Now the most important step is to create the model architecture.
So here you can see that we are defining the model architecture.
I have taken conv 2D layer with a filter size of 64 kernel size of three by three.
We have to pass the input shape in the first layer, the activation function.
I have taken it as ReLU and the padding equal to same.
Similarly, you can take any number of filters, any kernel size, any activation function, and you
can change the padding.
Also doing this is known as hyper parameter ization or hyper parameter tuning.
We have also applied some of the layers like max pool 2D layer with a pool size of two by two, we have
added a dropout layer.
Similarly, I have again added a conv 2D layer, max pool, 2D layer, and a dropout layer.
After the convolutional layers are finished, we apply a flatten layer to flatten the data and pass
it to the dense layer.
I have applied a dropout after the dense layer and the final layer, as you can see here.
It will always contain the number of neurons as the total number of classes, which are 43.
Here, as it is a multi-class classification problem, we will use the activation function as softmax.
Because softmax works on multi-class classification and gives a probability of all the classes.
So let's run this cell and make this architecture.
You can change this architecture according to your own requirements.
You can add as many maxpool layers as you want, and we can try to achieve a higher accuracy.
Let's move forward.
We have created the architecture.

So the loss here is sparse categorical because if we use categorical cross entropy it will not work
as the data is sparse.
It contains 43 classes which is a very diverse data.
So we will use the loss as sparse categorical.
We have used the optimizer as Adam and accuracy as the metrics.

Y train data and also use the validation data.

--- Creating ML Model ---

So this is the network architecture for our model.
Here you can see that we have used different types of layer according to their features.
Namely you can see that initially we have called the model as sequential.
So as we are creating the sequential model after that we have called a conv 2D layer with a filter size
eight, with number of filters eight and a filter size of three by three.
So a conv 2D layer, it is used to create a convolutional kernel that is convolved with the input layer
to produce the output tensor.
Here you can see that we have put the padding as same and input shape is the shape of the images.
So here you can see the shape of the images 224 by 224 by three.
So after that we are using a max pooling layer.
So it is a downsampling technique which takes out the maximum value over the window defined by pool
size.
So the pool size here we have defined is this as three by three.
Similarly, we are repeating the conv 2d max pooling for two more times.
After that, you can see that we are adding a flatten layer.
So a flatten layer.
What it does it flattens the input and creates a 1D output which is required for the dense layer after
that.
So a dense layer, it produces the output as the dot product of the input and the kernel.
Here you can see that we are using 32 neurons for the first dense layer.
And after that we are creating the final layer.
And in the last layer you can see that we are using softmax as the activation function because it is
a multi-class classification problem.
After that, in the end we are printing out the summary of this model.
So let's run this cell and find out the summary of our model that we have created
So here you can see it shows us that our model is sequential.
It shows out the parameters that will be used, that will be trained.
It shows us different layers that we have used.
And in the end, you can see that there are 338038 total parameters that will be trained.
So after that we will compile the model.
And while compiling the model we need to set the type of the loss which will be categorical cross entropy.
For this case along with that we need to set the optimizer which we are taking as Adam with the learning
rate of 0.0005.
And we are taking the metrics that we will measure that we will visualize as accuracy.
So let's run this cell and compile our model So after the compilation the main step is to fit the model.
So fitting the model with the data and finding out the accuracy at each epoch.
How our model is learning.
We need to run this command.
So now we are training our model on 50 epochs and a batch size of 128.
As you can see here, you can try for more number of epochs to increase the accuracy.
But here we can see that the model has already reached a very high accuracy, so we don't need to run
it for more.
During each epoch, we can see how the model is performed by viewing the training and validation accuracy.
So let's run this fit command and see the accuracy.
So while this is running, let me tell you about epochs and batch size.
So if you increase the number of epochs the accuracy increases.
But overfitting also increases if you see that the accuracy.
Of the training data and the validation accuracy have a very high difference, that is.
Accuracy of the training data is low and the validation accuracy is very high.
It means that model is getting overfitted.
And in order to remove overfitting from our model, we use regularization and dropout.
We add regularization and dropouts.
So here you can see that we reached an accuracy of 98.85 and a validation accuracy of 0.9077.
So you can remove the overfitting you see here using the dropouts or regularization.

law enforcement, etc. there is a sub domain in face detection that is face analysis where we help identify
which part of an image or maybe video should be focused on to determine age, gender, emotions, etc..
